## 0e. Candle evidence - RESEARCH EVIDENCE, NOT A SIGNAL
Patterns: candle patterns (displacement, engulfing, pin bar) and SMC events (smc_*: sweep of sell-side (bull) / buy-side (bear) liquidity, BOS, CHoCH with displacement, first retrace into a fair value gap).

If you had entered at the NEXT candle's open after each pattern, with a stop 1 ATR away: how often did price reach +1R / +2R / +3R **after costs** before the stop (max 30 candles)? **Random** = the same test on random candles (same coins, same direction, 10x as many). **Verdict** compares +1R with random: 'beats chance' only if better by more than 2 standard errors. **Stopped** = the stop was hit within the time limit (it can happen after +1R was reached, so the columns can add up to more than 100%). Many rows are compared at once, so an occasional 'beats chance' can still be luck - and none of this includes the other rules a real strategy needs.

## 0f. Market regime
The market's 'mood' per timeframe, from closed candles. Confidence = how much of the evidence agrees (strong / moderate / weak - never a %). **Permission:** LONG needs at least 2 of 1D/4H/1H bullish and no STRONG_BEAR on 1W (weekly veto); SHORT is the mirror image. *Regimes now gate every strategy: each trades only in its allowed regimes and with timeframe permission (strategy spec v3).*

| Coin | 1W | 1D | 4H | 1H | Permission |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **BTC** | WEAK_BULL (weak) | TRANSITION (weak) | EXPANSION down (strong) | STRONG_BEAR (moderate) | SHORT allowed (4H/1H bearish, 1W WEAK_BULL) |
| **ETH** | RANGE (weak) | TRANSITION (weak) | EXPANSION down (strong) | STRONG_BEAR (moderate) | SHORT allowed (4H/1H bearish, 1W RANGE) |
| **SOL** | UNCLEAR (weak) | STRONG_BEAR (strong) | EXPANSION down (strong) | STRONG_BEAR (moderate) | SHORT allowed (1D/4H/1H bearish, 1W UNCLEAR) |
| **BNB** | UNCLEAR (weak) | TRANSITION (weak) | EXPANSION down (strong) | STRONG_BEAR (moderate) | SHORT allowed (4H/1H bearish, 1W UNCLEAR) |
| **XRP** | UNCLEAR (weak) | STRONG_BEAR (strong) | STRONG_BEAR (moderate) | STRONG_BEAR (moderate) | SHORT allowed (1D/4H/1H bearish, 1W UNCLEAR) |
| **ZEC** | STRONG_BULL (weak) | RANGE (moderate) | STRONG_BEAR (strong) | WEAK_BEAR (moderate) | NO TRADE (weekly veto: 1W STRONG_BULL against 4H/1H bearish) |
| **SUI** | UNCLEAR (weak) | WEAK_BEAR (moderate) | EXPANSION down (moderate) | STRONG_BEAR (moderate) | SHORT allowed (1D/4H/1H bearish, 1W UNCLEAR) |

**BTC evidence** (most coins follow BTC):
- **1W WEAK_BULL (weak)** - for: EMA-fast rising (+1.5 ATR in 10 candles); swing structure up (HH/HL); candle size 0.71x normal, Bollinger width above 61% of the last 100 candles; volume 0.89x normal · against: EMAs not lined up (neutral); ADX 23 = in between (20-25); ADX 23 is close to a threshold
- **1D TRANSITION (weak)** - for: swing structure down (LH/LL); ADX 29 = strong trend; candle size 1.18x normal, Bollinger width above 84% of the last 100 candles · against: EMAs not lined up; EMA-fast flat (-1.0 ATR in 10 candles)
- **4H EXPANSION down (strong)** - for: close below EMA-fast below EMA-slow; EMA-fast falling (-1.7 ATR in 10 candles); swing structure down (LH/LL); ADX 63 = strong trend; candle size 1.63x normal, Bollinger width above 99% of the last 100 candles; volume 1.89x normal; range expansion + displacement / breakout down in the last 3 candles · against: -
- **1H STRONG_BEAR (moderate)** - for: close below EMA-fast below EMA-slow; EMA-fast falling (-1.6 ATR in 10 candles); swing structure down (LH/LL); ADX 47 = strong trend; volume 0.91x normal · against: candle size 1.63x normal, Bollinger width above 86% of the last 100 candles (unusually wild for a trend)

*Full evidence for every coin: `reports/regime.json`. Daily history: `memory/market_regime_log.md`.*

#### 1. BTC SHORT (futures only) · 4h · `donchian_breakout-VEXIT` v1.0 (PAPER_TRADING)
- **Signal candle closed:** 2026-11-14 23:59 UTC (1 candle(s) ago - still valid)
- **Entry zone:** 63,623.49 - 64,676.53 (don't chase if price already left this zone)
- **Stop-loss:** 66,782.59 (4.10% away)
- **TP1:** 58,884.85 (2.0R) -> close 50%, move stop to entry (breakeven)
- **TP2:** 56,252.27 (3.0R) -> close the rest
- **Hold time:** usually ~60.0 h; close anyway after 240.0 h
- **Early exit if:** close > prev(highest(high,10))
- **Position size** (account 1000 USDT, risk 0.5% = 5.00 USDT): buy **0.00189928 BTC** (~122 USDT)
- **Risk engine would block it live:** 14.10 opposing level between entry and TP1
- **Why:** Breakouts of a 20-candle range with 1.5x normal volume show new buyers stepping in. ADX > 20 confirms the market is trending, not chopping. This is the family of rules with the best published long-term evidence in crypto (trend following).
- **Rules that were true:** `htf_down`; `close < prev(lowest(low,20))`; `volume > vol_sma(20) * 1.5`; `adx(14) > 20`
- **Context:** regime EXPANSION, SHORT allowed, higher-TF trend DOWN, RSI(14) 15.9, volume 2.15x average, BTC 4H DOWN · 4 strategies agree
- **Backtest proof:** on BTC: 143 trades, 47% win, +0.52R avg · all coins: 821 trades, 41% win, +0.26R avg, PF 1.49

## 3. Strategy scoreboard (after fees)
**Status and long-history numbers** come from the daily research run (last run 2026-11-15 07:06 UTC); **Layer A** (the last 15 days) is recalculated every hour. Only trades inside each strategy's allowed regimes and with timeframe permission are counted.

- **VALIDATION** = long history (Layer B): ≥ 30 trades, ≥ +0.10R per trade (+0.02R per re-tuned version), profit factor ≥ 1.2, max drawdown ≤ 10R, profitable in both the develop and the validate part, and cost-viable (fees + slippage ≤ 0.25R, i.e. stop ≥ 4x the round-trip cost).
- **PAPER_TRADING** (automatic) = VALIDATION + walk-forward (≥ 3 of 5 windows profitable and together profitable) + edge on ≥ 3 coins + still profitable with costs +50% + every ±20% change still profitable + no overfitting flag + beats its control twin. Paper signals are logged and get PAPER emails (practice only, at most 3 an hour).
- **BACKTESTING** = not good enough (yet) · **FAILED** = enough trades and losing · **RETIRED** = paper results broke the limits; only a new version can be tested again.

### 3b. Strategy lifecycle and control twins
IDEA → FORMALIZED → BACKTESTING → VALIDATION → PAPER_TRADING (automatic) → APPROVED (only with your yes). Strategy versions tested so far: **141** (`memory/experiments.md`); full record per version and timeframe in `memory/strategy_registry.csv`.

**Trials counter:** 552 strategy / version / timeframe tests so far (`memory/trials.csv`). The more ideas are tested, the more one looks good by luck, so PAPER_TRADING now also needs a t-statistic of the average trade ≥ **3.74** (Bonferroni: family-wise false-winner rate 0.05 over 552 trials; with 1 trial it would be 1.65).

**Research run duration:** 37.8 min (budget 90 min).

**Lookahead / recursive check** (on BTC): 94 cards checked - history cut after 6 signal candles, and started 500 candles later; 0 BIASED (269.7 s).

**Monte Carlo** (1000 shuffles of each cell's trades): PAPER_TRADING also needs the 95% worst drawdown ≤ 8R.

### 3d. Why trades lose (failure attribution)
Every backtest trade gets reason tags by fixed rules (section 17; rules and numbers in `config.yaml` → `attribution`). A tag is **systematic** (✓) only if it is clearly more common among losing trades than among winning ones (more than 2 standard errors, at least 30 losses) - or, for tags that only exist for losers, if it is in at least 25% of them. **Best point of losers** (MFE) = how far the typical loser was in profit first; **worst point of winners** (MAE) = how much heat the typical winner took. Only strategy / timeframe tests with 30+ trades are shown.

- Win rate **40%**, average **+0.22R** per trade, total **+41.5R** (at 0.5% risk, +1R = +0.5% of account)

**Costs used in every backtest:** LONG and SHORT = OKX futures fees + funding (always charged, never received). Details in `config.yaml` → `costs`.
